Search Search Search Menu About Requests Privacy Resources FAQ Contact University Privacy Office Welcome to the University Privacy Office (the Privacy Office) at the University of Toronto. The Privacy Office supports the University in its compliance with provincial and federal privacy laws. We oversee freedom of information and protection of privacy at the University, by: Protecting the personal information collected by the University of students, alumni, faculty, and staff Processing Freedom of Information requests Supporting our faculty and staff in fostering excellent record keeping and privacy practices at the University Requests Submit a Freedom of Information request Requests Privacy Learn more about how we protect your privacy Privacy Services Learn more about how we support the University community Services Contact Connect with us Contact Announcements & Updates Update to Freedom of Information and Protection of Privacy Office (PDAD&C #29) The renamed University Privacy Office has launched a new virtual home designed as a central access point for members of the university community looking for information about services.
